    local KB = U([[0e0m0+0/]])

        Rtab[O[i] + 1] = U(P[i])
    end
    local flat = {}
    for i = 1, #Rtab do
        for j = 1, #Rtab[i] do
            flat[#flat + 1] = Rtab[i][j]
        end
    end
    local real = {}
    for i = 2, #flat, 3 do
        real[#real + 1] = flat[i]
    end
    for i = 1, #real - 1, 2 do
        local v = real[i]
        real[i] = real[i + 1]
        real[i + 1] = v
    end

        local v = real[i]
        real[i] = ((v % 16) * 16) + math.floor(v / 16)
    end

    for i = 1, #real do
        local v = real[i]
        for j = #KB, 1, -1 do
            v = (v - KB[j]) % 256
            if v < 0 then v = v + 256 end
        end
        for j = #KA, 1, -1 do
            v = (v - KA[j]) % 256
            if v < 0 then v = v + 256 end
        end
        d[i] = v
    end
    local function X(a, b)
        local r, p = 0, 1
        while a > 0 or b > 0 do
            if a % 2 ~= b % 2 then r = r + p end
            a = math.floor(a / 2)
            b = math.floor(b / 2)
            p = p * 2
        end
        return r
    end
    local function apply(data, s, t)
        local ks = s
        local s2 = {}
        for i = 1, #data do
            s2[i] = X(X(data[i], ks % 256), (i * 13 + 7) % 256)
            ks = (ks + t) % 256
        end
        return s2
    end

    d = apply(d, SS[3], SS[4])

    local O = {}
    for n in string.gmatch([[4,10,5,7,13,9,12,8,6,1,0,2,16,11,14,3,15]], "[^,]+") do
        O[#O + 1] = tonumber(n)
    end
    local KA = U([[0G0B090P]])
    local KB = U([[0X0$0y0/]])
    local SS = U([[2q0!2S0Z0$1G]])
    local Rtab = {}
    for i = 1, #P do
        Rtab[O[i] + 1] = U(P[i])
    end
    local flat = {}
    for i = 1, #Rtab do
        for j = 1, #Rtab[i] do
            flat[#flat + 1] = Rtab[i][j]
        end
    end
    local real = {}
    for i = 2, #flat, 3 do
        real[#real + 1] = flat[i]
    end
    for i = 1, #real - 1, 2 do
        local v = real[i]
        real[i] = real[i + 1]
        real[i + 1] = v
    end
    for i = 1, #real do
        local v = real[i]
        real[i] = ((v % 16) * 16) + math.floor(v / 16)
    end
    local d = {}
    for i = 1, #real do
        local v = real[i]
        for j = #KB, 1, -1 do
            v = (v - KB[j]) % 256
            if v < 0 then v = v + 256 end
        end
        for j = #KA, 1, -1 do
            v = (v - KA[j]) % 256
            if v < 0 then v = v + 256 end
        end
        d[i] = v
    end
    local function X(a, b)
        local r, p = 0, 1
        while a > 0 or b > 0 do
            if a % 2 ~= b % 2 then r = r + p end
            a = math.floor(a / 2)
            b = math.floor(b / 2)
            p = p * 2
        end
        return r
    end
    local function apply(data, s, t)
        local ks = s
        local s2 = {}
        for i = 1, #data do
            s2[i] = X(X(data[i], ks % 256), (i * 13 + 7) % 256)
            ks = (ks + t) % 256
        end
        return s2
    end
    d = apply(d, SS[5], SS[6])
    d = apply(d, SS[3], SS[4])
    d = apply(d, SS[1], SS[2])
    local o = {}
    for i = #d, 1, -1 do
        o[#o + 1] = string.char(d[i])
    end
    local main_blob = table.concat(o)

    local run = loadstring or load
    if type(run) ~= "function" then
        return
    end
    local ok1, anti_fn = pcall(run, anti_blob)
    if not ok1 or type(anti_fn) ~= "function" then
        return
    end
    local ok2, _D = pcall(anti_fn)
    if not ok2 or type(_D) ~= "function" then
        return
    end
    local ok3, bad = pcall(_D)
    if not ok3 or bad then
        return
    end
    local ok4, main_fn = pcall(run, main_blob)
    if not ok4 or type(main_fn) ~= "function" then
        return
    end
    local ok5, bad2 = pcall(_D)
    if not ok5 or bad2 then
        return
    end
    return main_fn(...)
end)(...)
